No escape hatch — no --happy-path-suffices-for-money, --skip-failure-scenarios, --defer-failure-path-tests, --criticality-not-applicable-without-reason, --closure-without-scenario-map flag.

§11.4.240 — Producer ≠ Verifier scope separation: the actor that produces a change/artifact/verdict cannot be the one that gates it — least privilege over cleverness (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every project MUST enforce STRUCTURAL SEPARATION between the actor that PRODUCES a change/artifact/verdict and the actor that GATES that same work — the producer cannot write, weaken, delete, or edit the acceptance tests, the oracle, the gate configuration, the verdict store, the paired §1.1 mutation, or any other check gating its own work; enforcement is by CAPABILITY (structurally cannot reach), never by INSTRUCTION (told not to reach), following the "least privilege over cleverness" principle (extraction line 1488 [un-resolvable offset per the §11.4.239 CITATION NOTE; MODULE CORRECTED 2026-08-20 — the quoted passage resolves to module 28, not module 27; verified locator module_28:393]: "the durable fix is least privilege over cleverness ... the load-bearing move is that the agent chasing the green gate cannot reach the gate, the oracle, or the evidence store"). (A) Four scopes MUST resolve to distinct actors — PRODUCER / ORACLE / GATE / VERIFIER; a two-scope-or-more collapse involving the producer is a §11.4.240 finding; (B) enforcement channels are filesystem scope / branch protection (§11.4.248 PROTECTED-SPEC + CODEOWNERS) / verdict-store isolation / clean-checkout execution isolation — NEVER prompts, policies, or social contracts (extraction line 5715 [un-resolvable offset per the §11.4.239 CITATION NOTE; module 34 attribution VERIFIED CORRECT 2026-08-20, verified locator module_34:87] catalogues "we told it in the prompt not to hardcode" as the shallow / red-flag answer); (C) three forbidden collapses named: PRODUCER=ORACLE (tautological testing), PRODUCER=GATE (weakened thresholds), PRODUCER=VERIFIER (self-certification); (D) composes with the existing verifier layer — §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdicts, §11.4.125/.142/.209 independent review, §11.4.146(D3) status custody, §11.4.135 release verdict coverage, §11.4.165 independent verifier agent, §11.4.245 oracle independence; (E) honest boundary — separation forbids the CLASS of self-certification, does NOT prove verifier correctness (§11.4.107(10) still binds). (F) Independence-tier vocabulary + Tier-C capability-separation triggers, degrading honestly on a single-uid host (extension, spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26). Clauses (A)-(E) state the SEPARATION PRINCIPLE in the abstract; this clause supplies the CLOSED VOCABULARY for recording HOW MUCH separation was ACHIEVED, plus the two triggers that raise the required strength — because claiming "capability separation" where only "instance separation" is achievable is itself the theatre this anchor exists to prevent. (1) Closed tier set: every accepted verdict's recorded basis of independence MUST name its tier from the CLOSED SET {instance, model, capability} — instance (a separate agent/process instance sharing no in-flight state with the producer), model (a DIFFERENT model), capability (a genuine host-level access-control boundary separating the producer's write scope from the verifier's). (2) Model diversity is PERMITTED, NEVER REQUIRED — the false-positive guard: a verdict from an independent INSTANCE running the SAME model as the producer MUST be ACCEPTED on that basis alone; a check that refuses same-model independent verdicts is the exact class of false-positive refusal §11.4.201 forbids. This does NOT weaken the constitution's OTHER, narrower model-substrate pins — §11.4.256 (translation review runs on a DIFFERENT model/provider than the translator) and §11.4.209 (every mandatory independent code-review runs on Opus at xhigh, no fallback model) both remain in force unmodified for their own domains. (3) Tier-C trigger 1 — producer holds write access to the evidence/verdict store: the verdict MUST be written through the MOST RESTRICTIVE separation genuinely available on the host, and the ACHIEVED separation (not the aspired-to one) MUST be reported. (4) Tier-C trigger 2 — the three named high-blast-radius seams: at release-tag, qa-deploy or manual-qa-handoff (or the consumer's declared equivalents per §11.4.35) a producer-authored verdict is REFUSED regardless of the write-access analysis. (5) No demand for capability outside the two triggers: at a seam matching NEITHER trigger a separate agent INSTANCE MUST SUFFICE and capability separation MUST NOT be demanded — over-applying Tier-C strength to every seam is itself the same false-positive-refusal violation. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): on a SINGLE-UID host capability separation CANNOT be established by any mechanism an agent can set up — ownership defeats access-control lists, the append-only attribute requires privilege the agent lacks, and any key the producer can use it can also read — so such a host reports instance HONESTLY and never capability, until the operator takes the one host-boundary action agents are correctly forbidden from creating (§11.4.66/§12); the field records WHAT WAS ACHIEVED, never what was ASPIRED to, and honest degradation IS the compliant path. Recommended mechanism gate CM-INDEPENDENCE-TIER-HONESTLY-RECORDED + paired §1.1 mutation (record capability where no verified host boundary exists → MUST FAIL; refuse a same-model independent verdict at an ordinary seam → MUST FAIL; accept a producer-authored verdict at release-tag/qa-deploy/manual-qa-handoff → MUST FAIL; golden-FALSE per §11.4.201(1): an honest instance-tier verdict at an ordinary seam MUST NOT fire it); gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.6/.20/.70/.92/.107(10)/.115(F)/.125/.134/.135/.142/.146(D3)/.165/.194/.201/.205/.209/.245/.248/.249 (mutual composes with §11.4.249 four-role + flight-recorder architecture per §11.4.209 reviewer MINOR-1 — §11.4.240 the separation principle, §11.4.249 the four-role taxonomy that instantiates it). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-240-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-PRODUCER-NOT-VERIFIER-SCOPE-SPLIT + CM-LEAST-PRIVILEGE-CAPABILITY-NOT-INSTRUCTION + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.240. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --producer-may-write-tests, --self-certification-OK, --exhortation-suffices-as-enforcement, --producer-authored-verdict-accepted, --capability-limit-optional, --policy-document-is-enforcement, --single-actor-for-all-scopes flag.

§11.4.241 — Illegal-state-unrepresentability preference (types > API-shape > lint > property-test > runtime-assert) (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every load-bearing invariant a change relies on MUST land at the STRONGEST enforcement rung the target language/toolchain supports, walking a closed ordered ladder — (1) TYPE SYSTEM (invalid state cannot be constructed: sum types / newtype wrappers / NonEmpty / opaque handles), (2) API SHAPE (only exposed constructor path enforces it: private constructors + smart factories, sealed hierarchies, capability tokens), (3) LINT (invalid pattern refused by tree before build), (4) PROPERTY-BASED TEST (invariant asserted across generator-driven input space), (5) RUNTIME ASSERTION (last-line guard raises when invalid state actually forms). Defect PREVENTABLE at rung N caught only at rung > N is a finding. Operator honest boundary 2026-08-15 — rungs 1-2 conditional on toolchain support (mypy present, TypeScript strict, Pydantic used), rungs 3-5 unconditional (every language has a linter + runtime). §1.1 mutation: drop the type constraint / relax PositiveInt to int / make private constructor public → compiler/type-checker/API gate FAILs to build; if it still builds, the rung was decoration. Composes §11.4.6 / §11.4.108 (pushes enforcement LEFT of Layer 1) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-241-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-ILLEGAL-STATE-LADDER-PREFERENCE + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.241. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --runtime-assert-suffices, --skip-type-preference, --dynamic-language-exempts-all-rungs, --property-test-in-lieu-of-type, --decorative-type-annotation-OK flag.

§11.4.242 — Bisection-not-blame regression cause-finding + failure modes (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: regression localisation FIRST-choice mechanism is BISECTION (git bisect, O(log N)) over the range from the last known-good tag (§11.4.114) to current HEAD, NOT blame-log walking (O(N) linear) NOR speculative root-cause hunting (unbounded). Bisection produces the introducing commit as CAPTURED EVIDENCE (§11.4.5) feeding §11.4.102 systematic-debugging's root-cause phase with the actual change in hand. MONOTONE-SEARCH INVARIANT: bisection valid only when observable is monotone across the range (broken stays broken); non-monotone (flaky, environment-dependent, timing-race) MUST stabilise first — bisection over flaky signal produces WRONG "guilty" commit with same confidence as correct one. FAILURE-MODE CATALOGUE (closed): (1) flaky/non-monotone observable, (2) build-broken commits (mistaken for "bad", use bisect skip), (3) merge-commit introductions (defect in conflict resolution not either parent), (4) external/environmental changes (upstream dep, OS update), (5) delayed-fuse introductions (latent commit + later trigger), (6) test-infra regressions masquerading as product regressions. git bisect run script MUST use exit-code discipline (0=good, 1-124=bad, 125=skip); mapping every non-zero to "bad" collapses the taxonomy. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.114 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-242-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-BISECTION-BEFORE-BLAME + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.242. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --blame-log-suffices, --skip-bisection, --flaky-observable-bisect-anyway, --run-script-exit-code-collapse-OK, --regression-guess-without-localisation flag.

§11.4.243 — Characterization / golden-master safety net before behaviour-changing modification of legacy (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: before ANY behaviour-changing modification to code that qualifies as legacy (lacks tests asserting observable behaviour, or written spec diverges from actual, or predates the constitution's TDD discipline, or the modifier cannot describe its behaviour from reading source), the modifier MUST FIRST author CHARACTERIZATION TESTS — tests asserting the CURRENT observable behaviour as GROUND TRUTH whether "correct" or not — and land them as a green baseline. Only AFTER the safety net exists may the modification proceed; characterization tests then act as the oracle catching unintended behaviour changes. Pattern originates in Feathers's WORKING EFFECTIVELY WITH LEGACY CODE + generalises GOLDEN-MASTER (record outputs across representative input space; assert future runs match). Characterization is DESCRIPTIVE not prescriptive — it captures WHAT IS not WHAT SHOULD BE; if current behaviour is subtly wrong, ASK operator per §11.4.66 whether modification should preserve or correct it (never silently). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.108 / §11.4.115 (RED-first — characterization is legacy counterpart) / §11.4.118 (sampled input space = honest gaps, not completeness) / §11.4.124 (investigate-before-remove — parallel discipline) / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 (test-first backfilled for legacy). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-243-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-CHARACTERIZATION-BEFORE-LEGACY-MODIFICATION + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.243. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --modify-untested-legacy-directly, --skip-characterization, --claim-tests-not-needed-because-obvious, --decorative-assertion-suffices, --refactor-without-safety-net flag.

§11.4.244 — Cross-boundary contract tests + can-i-deploy gate (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: changes modifying a boundary between independently-deployable components (service↔︎consumer, library↔︎dependents, DB schema↔︎readers/writers, message topic↔︎subscribers) MUST be covered by CONSUMER-DRIVEN CONTRACT TESTS on both sides AND a can-i-deploy gate MUST verify proposed provider version compatibility against every currently-deployed consumer version (and vice versa) BEFORE the change ships. CDC pattern: consumers publish assumptions they make about the provider (Pact / Spring Cloud Contract / gRPC schema-evolution); providers verify each consumer's contract against actual implementation; provider change breaking a live consumer's contract fails provider's build. CAN-I-DEPLOY: at deploy attempt, gate queries broker for compatibility matrix (P × C1 × C2 × C3); single red cell BLOCKS deploy naming incompatible pair, NEVER warning-with-continue; MISSING contract = first-class refusal reason (§11.4.201, not implicit "assume compatible"). SemVer becomes MEASURABLE: PATCH bump breaking contract = mis-labelled version. Backward-compatibility window declared per §11.4.35 (support current + previous N minor versions, deprecate with M weeks notice); consumers outside window flagged for §11.4.66 operator decision. §11.4.244 MERGES what the task brief labelled contract-tests + can-i-deploy — they are useless in isolation. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.108 (per-side four-layer) / §11.4.135 (contract tests as permanent regression guards) / §11.4.185 (composed-system manual QA still needed) / §11.4.201 (gate honesty). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-244-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-CONTRACT-TESTS-BOTH-SIDES + CM-CAN-I-DEPLOY-GATE-WIRED + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.244. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-contract-tests, --deploy-without-compatibility-check, --assume-compatible-when-broker-silent, --patch-may-break-contract, --contract-tests-optional-for-internal-boundaries flag.

§11.4.245 — Oracle-problem-first test authoring: identify the oracle BEFORE authoring the test (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every test MUST FIRST identify its ORACLE — the mechanism deciding PASS vs FAIL for the input it drives — and the oracle MUST be DIFFERENT from the code under test AND from the test's own assertion mechanism. Authoring without explicit oracle identification produces the "test agrees with code" anti-pattern (test asserts what code happens to produce, so it passes as long as code produces something, never catches wrong-output). ORACLE STRATEGY CLOSED SET, each test's oracle NAMED in source (comment/docstring/tag): (1) SPECIFIED (spec/RFC/standard names expected output), (2) DERIVED (independent reference implementation), (3) METAMORPHIC (metamorphic relations between related inputs), (4) GOLDEN-MASTER / CHARACTERIZATION (past captured behavior — §11.4.243), (5) INVARIANT (universal property — sort output sorted + permutation of input; serialize-deserialize round-trip = original), (6) STATISTICAL (distribution properties for stochastic outputs, never specific value), (7) HUMAN (§11.4.185 manual QA). NO other classes. STRUCTURAL INDEPENDENCE: oracle's SOURCE of expected value must not depend on code being tested — different implementation / spec / previous run / mathematical property / inverse operation / human. Reviewer per §11.4.194 verifies (i) NAMED strategy, (ii) structural independence, (iii) paired §1.1 mutation flips the oracle. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) (self-validated analyzers — analyzer-as-oracle) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 / §11.4.243. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-245-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-ORACLE-STRATEGY-NAMED-AND-INDEPENDENT + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.245. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --test-agrees-with-code, --skip-oracle-identification, --oracle-is-the-code, --assertion-is-oracle, --implicit-oracle-OK flag.

§11.4.246 — Reproducible + hermetic builds + supply-chain integrity at SLSA Build Level 2 as fleet-wide minimum (operator decision, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every consuming project's build pipeline MUST produce REPRODUCIBLE + HERMETIC artifacts and meet SLSA Build Level 2 as fleet-wide MINIMUM (operator decision 2026-08-15 — L3+ tracked as backlog per §11.4.197). REPRODUCIBLE = same source at same time on any conformant host produces byte-identical artifacts (or diff-set fully accounted for by declared non-determinisms pinned to canonical values). HERMETIC = no undeclared inputs (every dep version-pinned + lockfile-hashed, every tool version declared, no unpinned public-network reach at build, no host-env leakage). SLSA-L2 = signed provenance describing source URI + commit hash + builder identity + invocation + artifact hash, on hosted tamper-resistant build platform (boba already satisfies ancillary: SSH-only §2.1, rootless podman §11.4.161, fixed-tag pins §11.4.30/§11.4.77). Every consumer MAINTAINS docs/security/SLSA_LEVEL.md (or equivalent per §11.4.35) recording CURRENT L level with cited evidence + TARGET L + tracked §11.4.197 upgrade items — CURRENT below L2 without tracked item is violation. SUPPLY-CHAIN EXTENSION: deps either vendored (checked in hash-verified), from provenance-attested source (npm/PyPI/crates.io as they add SLSA), OR mirrored through hash-pinning local registry — an unattested public source is integrity risk. Threat model NARROW: covers source→artifact chain, does NOT cover malicious commit landing in source (§11.4.142 review) nor compromised dep source (supply-chain extension) nor bug-free artifact (§11.4.108 four-layer + §11.4.185 manual QA). Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.30 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.108 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.161 / §11.4.184 / §11.4.185 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.209. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-246-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-BUILD-REPRODUCIBILITY + CM-BUILD-HERMETICITY + CM-SLSA-L2-PROVENANCE-VERIFIED + CM-SUPPLY-CHAIN-DEPS-PROVENANCE-ATTESTED + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.246. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-reproducibility, --allow-network-during-build, --no-provenance, --slsa-optional, --below-l2-OK, --unpinned-dependency-OK, --host-env-may-leak-into-build flag.

§11.4.247 — Composite-output layer-move must move every layer (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: whenever a change MOVES a system-boundary layer (service migrates hosts, endpoint URL changes, API version bumps, DB schema shifts, message topic renames, ingress path re-routes, storage bucket moves), the change MUST move EVERY LAYER of the composite output atomically or via explicit compatibility window — NEVER piecemeal. Recurring failure: endpoint moves /v1/foo → /v2/foo; server handler + client call-site moved; load balancer + CDN + API gateway + OpenAPI spec + SDK stubs + integration tests + metrics + traces + logs + dashboards + alerts + docs still reference /v1/foo; operator declares victory; residuals silently break when /v1/foo shim retires. LAYER TAXONOMY per §11.4.35 baseline: (i) server (handler + route + OpenAPI/proto/gRPC descriptor), (ii) client (call-sites + SDK stubs + integration tests), (iii) infrastructure (LB + ingress + API gateway + CDN + service-mesh + DNS), (iv) data stores (cached keys + DB URL rows + MQ subscriptions), (v) observability (metric labels + span names + log fields + dashboards + alerts + SLOs), (vi) documentation (user + dev + runbook + README + cookbook), (vii) tests (integration + e2e + contract per §11.4.244 + load + smoke). Consumer provides scripts/audit/layer_move_completeness.sh reporting residual references delta; change proposing move MUST show ZERO residuals OR explicit compatibility-window plan per residual with tracked §11.4.197 items. COMPATIBILITY-WINDOW: old value keeps working alongside new until stated date/version/event, deprecation warnings named per caller, tracked deadline, clean removal at expiry — no "clean up later." Composes §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.111 / §11.4.118 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.191 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.238 (residual = coverage escape) / §11.4.244. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-247-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-LAYER-MOVE-COMPLETENESS-CHECK + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.247. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --partial-layer-move-OK, --skip-completeness-check, --residuals-cleaned-up-later, --observability-layer-optional, --doc-move-can-lag flag.

§11.4.248 — Flaky-test quarantine + protected-regression-spec gate via [PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN] tag + CODEOWNERS required reviewer (operator decision, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: flaky tests are the SILENT ANTI-BLUFF DECAY vector (every flake conditions team to ignore red until real regression is dismissed as "probably flaky, re-run"). Two disciplines bound: (A) QUARANTINE — mechanical detection (test-history aggregator flags two-consecutive-run-different-verdict OR rerun-after-red pattern) moves flaky test to tests/quarantine/ (per §11.4.35), opens tracked §11.4.197 stabilisation deadline, blocking suite runs WITHOUT quarantined test so green means green; deadline past = §11.4.66 extension or violation; (B) PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC — regression tests codifying known-broken defect fixes (per §11.4.135 permanent-guard suite) MAY be tagged # [PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN] (target-language comment equivalent) referencing the workable-item ATM-NNN (§11.4.54) they guard, marking test as load-bearing regression guard whose modification requires elevated review. Tag applied deliberately per test (not blanket — that voids the discipline). CODEOWNERS-REQUIRED-REVIEWER GATE: consuming project's CODEOWNERS declares modifications to [PROTECTED-SPEC]-tagged tests OR regression/**/* path scope require designated required-reviewer approval BEFORE merge; composes with §2.1 SSH + §11.4.113 no-force-push (gate cannot be silently bypassed); audit trail in PR review thread + commit history. Gate CATCHES change so reviewer distinguishes STRENGTHEN (approve) vs FIX-DEFECT-IN-TEST (approve after verifying ATM-NNN's defect not silently re-introduced) vs WEAKEN/REMOVE (refuse or §11.4.66 escalate) — makes modification VISIBLE not preventable. Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.50 (ideal empty quarantine) / §11.4.54 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.226. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-248-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-FLAKY-TEST-QUARANTINE-WIRED + CM-PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC-GATE + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.248. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --rerun-until-green, --quarantine-permanent, --protected-spec-review-optional, --codeowners-skip, --blanket-protect-everything, --tag-without-atm-id flag.

§11.4.249 — Producer ≠ oracle ≠ gate ≠ verifier + flight recorder (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every load-bearing quality mechanism has FOUR distinct roles that MUST be structurally separate — (1) PRODUCER (code computing/emitting/building output), (2) ORACLE (mechanism deciding correctness per §11.4.245), (3) GATE (seam consuming oracle verdict to allow/block/proceed), (4) VERIFIER (mechanism auditing gate's decision — paired §1.1 mutation proving gate can refuse a bad output). Collapsing any two produces specific bluff class: producer=oracle (§11.4.245 oracle-agrees-with-code); oracle=gate (refuses to say "cannot decide" defaults to allow, §11.4.201(6) FALSE-NULL); gate=verifier (self-auditing gate — §11.4.142 self-review anti-pattern applied). FLIGHT RECORDER: mechanism CAPTURING producer inputs + oracle verdict + gate decision + verifier audit as durable replayable artifact (structured JSONL event stream per §11.4.116, content-addressed per §11.4.207) so post-hoc "why did this go through" has evidence not shrug. Recorder is NOT the gate — a gate reading its own recorder to decide collapses gate + verifier + recorder. DETECTABLE COLLAPSES reviewer per §11.4.194 scans for: (1) producer=oracle (test's assertion calls code being tested), (2) oracle=gate (no "cannot decide" branch, defaults PASS), (3) gate=verifier (meta-test IS the gate — mutation making gate always-pass ALSO makes meta-test always-pass; meta-test MUST live OUTSIDE gate), (4) recorder=decision-substrate (gate uses recorder contents as decision input — precedent-based gating not authorised). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.116 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.207 / §11.4.240 (producer↔︎verifier separation principle — the WHY behind this four-role architecture) / §11.4.245. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-249-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-ROLE-SEPARATION-DECLARED + CM-FLIGHT-RECORDER-CAPTURED + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.249. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --collapse-producer-oracle, --gate-self-audits, --skip-flight-recorder, --oracle-defaults-pass, --decision-without-recorded-rationale flag.

§11.4.250 — Heuristic-tower signals a primitive defect: when N heuristic layers stack to compensate, the primitive is broken (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: codebase accumulating TOWER of heuristics compensating for same underlying issue (retry-with-backoff + connection-pool-warmup + DNS-cache-flush + health-check-with-startup-grace — all guarding one flaky DB connection) IS the diagnostic: PRIMITIVE is defective, every layer papers over a manifestation without fixing cause. Recognition signals: (i) each layer justification "we added because sometimes X happens" — reactive, (ii) removing any layer INCREASES failures BUT keeping all shows occasional residual, (iii) no single layer owns responsibility, (iv) layers added at different times by different authors per specific incidents, (v) monotone layer-count growth over time — debugging session for residual adds layer N+1 rather than fixing 1..N. DISTINCT from defense-in-depth: defense-in-depth is deliberate + each layer targets DIFFERENT failure class + designed together not accreted + removing layer produces SPECIFIC different failure not "same failure more often". SYSTEMATIC-DEBUGGING RESPONSE on tower depth ≥ 2: (1) STOP adding layers, (2) INVOKE §11.4.102 systematic-debugging on PRIMITIVE, (3) proceed root-cause / pattern / hypothesis / implementation phases, (4) fix primitive, (5) REMOVE layers one at a time verifying with §11.4.5 evidence, (6) commit each removal per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence citing primitive fix. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 (outer layer masks §11.4.201(6) false-null) / §11.4.226. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-250-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-HEURISTIC-TOWER-TRIGGERS-PRIMITIVE-DEBUG + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.250. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --add-another-heuristic-layer, --skip-primitive-debug, --tower-is-defense-in-depth-without-justification, --reactive-mitigation-OK, --layer-per-incident flag.

§11.4.251 — Byte-identical-fork prohibition + role-as-data-pack (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: when project needs would be met by TWO NEAR-IDENTICAL CODE COPIES differing only in configuration / data / role-specific parameters (prod + staging with hardcoded hostnames; reader + writer with different flag values; web + mobile with different feature flags), copies MUST NOT be maintained as parallel forks. Instead ONE code copy retained + role-specific values extracted into DATA PACK (role-config file / env var set / capability descriptor / feature-flag bundle) consumed by the one code to instantiate as either role. Anti-pattern: FORK-AND-DIVERGE (copies drift as one patched not other, until drift = defect surface no one owns). DETECTION: reviewer or mechanical scan finds byte-identical fork when diff shows every difference is a config value (hostname/port/flag/label/env/credential-ref) NOT behavioral, common git-history ancestor, > 90% byte identity; per §11.4.35 threshold-DATA. EXTRACTION: (1) IDENTIFY configuration surface (closed value set differing), (2) EXTRACT to data pack per role, (3) REFACTOR single code copy to consume at instantiation (constructor injection / factory / config-loader-at-startup), (4) DELETE one fork, (5) VERIFY per §11.4.5 both role instantiations produce same observable behavior their forks previously produced (§11.4.243 characterization before refactor), (6) commit extraction per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence. LEGITIMATE FORKS: temporary migration (tracked §11.4.197 end date), experimental / A-B (bounded end date), truly divergent behavior (copies differ in algorithm/protocol/data-model not just config). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.28 (decoupling — role-configurable code shared across consumers) / §11.4.35 (data-pack location) / §11.4.124 / §11.4.177 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.243. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-251-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-NO-BYTE-IDENTICAL-FORK + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.251. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --fork-for-role-OK, --skip-data-pack-extraction, --copy-and-modify-config, --duplicate-code-per-environment, --near-identical-forks-are-fine flag.

§11.4.252 — Fail-closed-on-dangerous-combination for mutating/credential surfaces (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every code path COMBINING ≥ 2 dangerous capabilities MUST FAIL CLOSED — refuse operation unless every capability's precondition verifiably satisfied — NEVER fail open on ambiguous/missing/unresolvable input. Security-engineering generalisation of §11.4.201 guard-honesty applied to code paths where false-negative blast radius dwarfs operator inconvenience of false-positive. DANGEROUS-COMBINATION TAXONOMY: (1) MUTATION of shared resource (DB / filesystem / container state / config), (2) UNTRUSTED INPUT (user string / network bytes / external env var / unverified file), (3) CREDENTIAL ACCESS (secret read / authenticated API call / signing key use), (4) EXTERNAL SIDE EFFECT (email / webhook / MQ publish / payment endpoint), (5) SHELL / EXEC (subshell / binary with user-influenced args / template eval with user values), (6) IRREVERSIBLE (delete / drop / truncate — §11.4.113 force-push already absolutely forbidden). Any path combining ≥ 2 = dangerous-combination, MUST fail closed. INVARIANT: (1) VERIFY every precondition (authorization + input shape + credential presence + target identity), (2) REFUSE when ANY unverifiable with ERROR EXIT naming specific unresolved precondition, (3) EMIT captured evidence per §11.4.5 of refusal (log reason + resolved inputs + unresolved precondition + caller identity), (4) NEVER default to allow/proceed/retry-with-relaxed on ambiguity — §11.4.101 conservative-safe default = REFUSE. FAIL-OPEN ANTI-PATTERNS refused: catch { /* ignore */ }, credential = credential || default, if (!valid) { log("warn"); return proceed(input); }, target = user_input || default_target, unbounded retry { dangerous(); }. Distinct from graceful degradation (cache-miss returning cache-miss response is legitimate — NOT dangerous combination). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.10 (credentials specialisation) / §11.4.66 (operator escalation when refuse stuck) / §11.4.101 / §11.4.113 (max instantiation) / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-252-PROPAGATION + recommended gate CM-DANGEROUS-COMBINATION-FAIL-CLOSED + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.252. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --fail-open-on-error, --swallow-exception, --default-missing-credential, --proceed-on-ambiguous-input, --retry-until-succeeds flag.

§11.4.253 — Idempotency under retry + DB-level durable uniqueness guard (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every retryable operation (network calls with auto-retry, at-least-once MQ consumers, cron with overlapping schedules, HTTP endpoints with client-retry, DB transactions with retry-on-serialisation-failure, deploy pipelines with rerun capability) MUST BE IDEMPOTENT: N executions produce same observable end state as one. Idempotency ENFORCED at LOWEST LAYER where "same end state" is durably provable — typically DATABASE UNIQUENESS CONSTRAINT on caller-supplied idempotency key, NEVER only application-layer check-then-insert (races). Prevents DOUBLE-CHARGE / DOUBLE-EMAIL / DOUBLE-DELETE / DOUBLE-EFFECT bugs. IDEMPOTENCY-KEY DISCIPLINE: every retryable op accepts (or generates + passes forward) unique identifier naming THIS logical op; persistence layer has UNIQUE INDEX / CONSTRAINT on key column; second attempt REFUSED by DB at durable level not application logic reading-then-writing; refusal caught by caller as "duplicate — already succeeded" = SUCCESS (op happened, no further retry). ANTI-PATTERNS: (i) check-then-insert without unique index races, (ii) app-level dedup table read without spanning-transaction, (iii) key scoped to app instance (in-memory not shared), (iv) effect persisted BEFORE key (crash between = double-effect on retry), (v) no key at all + "the effect is 'set X to V' so redoing is fine" (safe for pure sets, NOT for INSERTs / MQ emissions / external-system side effects), (vi) idempotency-key expires — converts to time-bounded-idempotent = §11.4.6 mis-labelling if caller expects unbounded. CHAOS VERIFICATION per §11.4.85: (1) SIMULTANEOUS RETRIES (same key from 2 concurrent callers), (2) MID-OPERATION CRASHES (kill receiver between "check exists" and "insert effect"), (3) RETRY AFTER APPARENT SUCCESS (caller believes fail, receiver persisted, retry MUST recognise duplicate). External-system side effects (Stripe/PayPal charging card + local DB write): idempotency key MUST pass through to external; if external does not support, op is not fully idempotent — label honestly per §11.4.6. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.85 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.252. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-253-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-IDEMPOTENCY-DB-LEVEL-UNIQUE-GUARD + CM-IDEMPOTENCY-CHAOS-VERIFIED + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.253. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --check-then-insert-OK, --application-dedup-suffices, --skip-idempotency-chaos-test, --claim-idempotent-without-key, --expire-idempotency-marker-silently flag.

§11.4.254 — Boot-time invariant assertion + capability matrix (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Compact summary: every service / process / long-running executable MUST assert BOOT-TIME INVARIANTS at startup — required env vars present + valid; required config files parseable + declared schema version matches; required deps reachable; required credentials present + not-expired; required schema migrations applied; required feature-flag states declared; required host resources present (disk / memory / thread limits per §12.12 / port availability) — and FAIL FAST + LOUD if any violated, BEFORE accepting request / performing work. Prevents "service silently starts with broken invariant + serves requests that fail deep in request path hours after boot." INVARIANT CLASSES: (i) environment, (ii) configuration, (iii) dependencies, (iv) credentials, (v) schema, (vi) capabilities, (vii) host. FAIL-FAST-LOUD: (1) EMIT structured error to stderr + captured log naming specific failed invariant (env var name / config path / dep URL / credential file / schema version), (2) EXIT non-zero (traditionally 78 EX_CONFIG for config, consumer supplies per §11.4.35), (3) DO NOT accept requests / begin main event loop / bind listening port. Service booting partially + serving requests-that-will-fail is worse than refusing to start — partial-boot masquerades healthy long enough for load balancer to route traffic. Dual to §11.4.252 fail-closed on dangerous combinations: both refuse to proceed on unresolvable preconditions. CAPABILITY MATRIX: consumer's docs/services/CAPABILITY_MATRIX.md (per §11.4.35) declares per service (i) boot-time invariants, (ii) runtime capabilities OFFERED (endpoints / topics / callable functions), (iii) runtime capabilities REQUIRED from other services. Regenerated / verified on every commit per §11.4.106 docs-chain + §11.4.86 fingerprint. Undeclared endpoints emitted = violation; silent upstream deps not declared = violation (invisible to §11.4.244 contract testing). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.86 / §11.4.106 / §11.4.108 Layer 3 (boot IS runtime-on-clean-target check) / §11.4.128 / §11.4.244 (contract tests consume matrix) / §11.4.252 / §12.12. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-254-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-BOOT-TIME-INVARIANT-ASSERTED + CM-CAPABILITY-MATRIX-PRESENT-AND-IN-SYNC + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.254. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-boot-time-assertion, --boot-partial-OK, --default-missing-env-silently, --capability-matrix-optional, --service-with-undeclared-dependency-OK flag.

§11.4.262 — Machine-created evidence at every gate: every claim of works/passes/verified cites captured machine-derived machine-verifiable evidence, no operator eyeballing, no narrative-only PASS (BACKGROUND :: REMINDER :: IMPORTANT operator mandate, 2026-08-15). STRENGTHENS the §11.4 covenant preamble from CAPTURED to CAPTURED-AND-MACHINE-VERIFIABLE evidence, applying §11.4.107(10) self-validating analyzers UNIVERSALLY (not only AV playback). THREE-GATE COVERAGE: (1) CONFIRMATION (source review + static + clash §11.4.110), (2) VALIDATION (§11.4.108 four layers each with its own machine verdict + artifact), (3) VERIFICATION (§11.4.185 human + §11.4.236 machine sufficiency, each with captured evidence). MACHINE-CREATED four properties: produced by the gate not the tester, schema-parseable/machine-verifiable (JSON verdict, JSONL event stream, OCR/vision oracle per §11.4.107/§11.4.117, audio RMS/loudness), content-addressed, semantic-class-matched (§11.4.226 wrong-layer echo forbidden). Every evidence analyzer golden-good/golden-bad/negative-control validated per §11.4.107(10); analyzer that passes its golden-bad = bluff itself + release-blocker (§11.4.201). Four-layer binding with §11.4.108 — every layer PASS cites machine evidence PRODUCED AT THAT LAYER; higher layer citing lower layer only = §11.4.226 wrong-layer violation. §11.4.263 — Process-group signal-safety mandate: NEVER signal pgid ≤ 1, NEVER trust a mock-derived pid/pgid — validate as int > 1 before every killpg / kill(-pid, sig) / pkill / killall call (BOB-126 forensic anchor, 2026-08-19). Compact summary: SEVEN forced-logout incidents on the operator's workstation over ~48h (BOB-116/120/123/124/125/126) traced by kernel audit trail to a single Python test — AsyncMock().pid unset → MagicMock.__int__ == 1 default → os.getpgid(1) == 1 → os.killpg(1, SIGKILL) → glibc → kill(-1, SIGKILL) = SIGKILL every UID-1000 process (systemd user@1000 manager, gnome-shell, tmux, ssh, browsers, Claude Code) — with contextlib.suppress(Exception) swallowing nothing because the syscall SUCCEEDED. Universal mandate: (A) NEVER signal pgid ≤ 1 in any language — killpg(1,sig) == kill(-1,sig) == the disaster syscall on Linux/BSD/macOS; (B) validate isinstance(pid,int) and pid > 1 AND isinstance(pgid,int) and pgid > 1 before every process-group signal call (Python os.killpg, Go syscall.Kill(-pgid,sig), Rust nix killpg, Bash kill -<pgid>/pkill -g, C killpg/kill(-pid,sig)); (C) TESTS mocking subprocess/proc objects MUST explicitly set mock.pid = <int> (never rely on mock library defaults) AND patch the killpg call itself AND assert killpg was called with pgid > 1; (D) DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH four-layer coverage per §11.4.4(b) — pre-build grep gate, post-build CodeGraph query, runtime kernel audit rule syscall=kill a0=0xffffffff a1=9, paired §1.1 mutation regression test; (E) honest boundary — hardens process-group signal APIs; does NOT prohibit legitimate start_new_session=True child-owned groups (guard still cheap); does NOT prevent kernel OOM-killer / systemd-oomd (§12.6 + §11.4.225 apply); (F) standing default from first prompt — every code review scans for killpg calls + verifies guard, every subagent writing subprocess-cleanup code includes the guard, every test-writing subagent includes explicit mock.pid. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.1/§11.4.4(b)/§11.4.5/§11.4.6/§11.4.85/§11.4.107(10)/§11.4.115/§11.4.126/§11.4.201/§11.4.225/§11.4.226. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-263-PROPAGATION + recommended gates CM-KILLPG-PGID-GUARD + CM-TEST-MOCK-PID-EXPLICIT-INT + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.263. Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no --skip-killpg-guard, --mock-pid-optional, --killpg-1-is-ok, --kill-minus-1-permitted, --suppress-catches-syscall, --test-may-not-explicit-pid flag.

§11.4.255 — Mandatory HelixTranslate canonical translation pipeline (User mandate, 2026-06-25). Compact summary: ALL translations of ANY content — UI strings, documents, articles, CVs, READMEs, marketing copy, in-app messages, release notes, legal text, ANY human-language string rendered in a non-source language — MUST be produced by HelixTranslate (git@github.com:HelixDevelopment/HelixTranslate.git) driven through the canonical universal translation pipeline shipped in the constitution submodule at scripts/translation/ (translate-pipeline.sh + render-articles.sh) — a hard, non-negotiable constraint. FORBIDDEN as §11.4.255 violations: (a) hand-translation — no human-authored, manually edited, or "good enough" hand-written translation of any content, ever; the engine is the single source of every translated string; (b) other engines — no Google Translate, DeepL, raw ChatGPT/Claude prompting, OS translation APIs, library i18n auto-translators, or any engine other than HelixTranslate's unified-translator; there is exactly one engine; (c) silent fallback — no quiet substitution of the source string, no empty/partial output passed off as a translation, no swallowed engine error, no absence-of-error PASS (§11.4/§11.4.1); on exhaustion of all configured providers the pipeline MUST FAIL LOUD (non-zero exit, destination NOT written) so the caller detects it, a missing translation being a hard error never a degraded-but-shipped string — provider-to-provider failover WITHIN HelixTranslate (primary → fallback, with logged, evidenced retries) is NOT a silent fallback and is permitted, while falling back to ANY non-HelixTranslate source is forbidden. Mandatory validation — every generated translation MUST be validated for accuracy with real (physical) captured evidence per §11.4.5/§11.4.69/§11.4.107, no metadata-only / config-only / grep-without-runtime PASS (§11.4/§11.4.1), no false results, no bluff of any kind (§11.4.6); the per-file run log (provider used, byte counts, retries, success/failure) IS the evidence artifact, and an unvalidated or unevidenced translation is treated as ABSENT. Explicit provider routing — provider + model routing MUST be explicit and recorded, never implicit/defaulted-in-the-dark; the canonical routing is provider groq / model llama-3.3-70b-versatile with documented failover to provider mistral / model mistral-large-latest, the provider that actually produced each output MUST be recorded in the per-file log, and any deviation from the canonical routing MUST be stated explicitly in configuration and logged. Canonical pipeline location — scripts/translation/translate-pipeline.sh (universal Markdown / plain-text + article-frontmatter-preserving translator) + scripts/translation/render-articles.sh (article-source → HTML fragment renderer); both reusable verbatim across projects, the engine binary supplied by the consuming project via HELIX_TRANSLATE_BIN and built from the HelixTranslate repository. Rationale — a single, evidenced, fail-loud translation engine guarantees every translated string in the fleet has a known provenance, a reproducible pipeline, and a captured accuracy proof, eliminating the silent drift, inconsistent quality, and untraceable "where did this string come from" failures that hand-translation and ad-hoc engines produce: one engine, one pipeline, one evidence trail. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — a platform-neutral, language-neutral translation discipline reusable by ANY project; the consuming project supplies its concrete source files, target languages, and the HelixTranslate engine build per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4 / §11.4.1 / §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.7 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.107. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-255-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.255) + recommended gate CM-MANDATORY-HELIXTRANSLATE-PIPELINE + paired §1.1 meta-test mutation (gate-code = separate work item). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.255. Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no --allow-hand-translation, --other-engine-OK, --skip-translation-validation, --silent-fallback flag exists.

§11.4.256 — Mandatory independent per-language translation review (User mandate, 2026-06-25). Compact summary: every translated artifact produced under §11.4.255 MUST, BEFORE it is shipped / committed / deployed, be INDEPENDENTLY REVIEWED for quality by a per-language review agent — a hard, non-negotiable gate that composes with, and NEVER replaces, the §11.4.255 generation mandate. Requirements: (a) Independence — the reviewer MUST be a different model/provider than the one that produced the translation (e.g. translator mistral/mistral-large-latest → reviewer groq/llama-3.3-70b-versatile, or any distinct verified model); a model reviewing its own output is NOT an independent review and is forbidden. (b) Per-language specialization — the reviewer MUST act as a NATIVE speaker / linguistic reviewer of the SPECIFIC target language, evaluating that language's grammar, orthography, and correct script (Cyrillic for ru/be/kk, Arabic for ar/fa, Han/Kana/Hangul/Devanagari for zh/ja/ko/hi, Latin for sr/de/es/fr/tr, etc.). (c) Full criteria — each review MUST judge accuracy/fidelity, fluency/naturalness, completeness (nothing omitted or invented), correct script + orthography, and absence of untranslated source leftovers (proper nouns / URLs / code excepted). (d) Strict machine-readable verdict + evidence — each review MUST emit a structured PASS/FAIL verdict with per-criterion scores and an issues list, persisted as a physical evidence artifact (§11.4.5 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.107); a FAIL — or an ERROR / unavailable-reviewer — BLOCKS the content and only a real PASS may ship, with no metadata-only / absence-of-error PASS (§11.4 / §11.4.1) and no bluff (§11.4.6). (e) Remediation loop — a FAIL routes the artifact back through the §11.4.255 pipeline (re-translate / multipass-polish) and is re-reviewed, iterating until PASS, NEVER hand-patched to pass (hand-translation remains forbidden under §11.4.255). Canonical tooling — scripts/translation/review-translations.sh (per-language review driver) + scripts/translation/review_translation.py (independent reviewer engine emitting strict JSON verdicts); reusable verbatim across projects, the consuming project supplying its source/translation file pairs and a reviewer provider DISTINCT from its translator. Rationale — §11.4.255 guarantees a translation's provenance and a fail-loud pipeline, but not its linguistic quality; an independent, native-level, per-language reviewer with a captured PASS/FAIL verdict is what turns "a string was produced" into "a string was verified correct", making translation quality an auditable fact rather than an assumption. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — a platform-neutral, language-neutral translation-quality-assurance discipline reusable by ANY project that ships translated content. Composes §11.4.255 (the translation it gates) / §11.4 / §11.4.1 / §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.107 / §11.4.134 (rock-solid evidence). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-256-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.256) + recommended gate CM-MANDATORY-TRANSLATION-REVIEW + paired §1.1 meta-test mutation (gate-code = separate work item). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.256. Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no --skip-translation-review, --self-review-OK, --accept-unreviewed-translation flag exists.

§11.4.264 — Build once, promote ONE immutable content-addressed artifact through every environment; never rebuild per stage (research-derived, 2026-08-20). Compact summary: exactly ONE artifact is produced per release candidate and THAT artifact is promoted through every environment; rebuilding per stage silently BREAKS THE CHAIN OF EVIDENCE, because every gate that ran against the staging artifact measured a DIFFERENT binary than production runs — making each of those green verdicts a §11.4 PASS-bluff about the shipped artifact (corpus module 31: "The thing you tested must be the thing you ship … Rebuilding per environment silently breaks the chain of evidence"; red flag: "'We rebuild for production to be safe.'"). (A) BUILD ONCE per release candidate under §11.4.246 (reproducible + hermetic + SLSA-L2); determinism makes a rebuild detectably equivalent, it is never a licence to rebuild instead of promote. (B) IDENTITY IS A CONTENT ADDRESS — a digest, never a mutable tag ("tags get re-pushed; digests cannot change without changing the digest"; the attack defended is "the same version, different bytes"), composing §11.4.111 and §11.4.200 (identity read BACK from the intended target). (C) PROMOTION IS A VERIFICATION — "verify this exact digest is authentic and approved for the next environment," not "make a new thing and hope it's equivalent"; signature/SBOM/provenance travel WITH the bytes. (D) ENVIRONMENT CONFIG IS INJECTED AT DEPLOY, NEVER BAKED AT BUILD (baking per-environment config re-creates the per-stage rebuild; §11.4.10 still forbids baking a secret). (E) ROLLBACK IS DETERMINISTIC BY CONSEQUENCE — point at the previous known-good digest, "not a rebuild-from-source scramble under incident pressure" — the precondition that makes §11.4.265's auto-abort a real undo. (F) REBUILD-TO-DODGE IS GATE-EVASION — "re-running until green, or rebuilding to get a different result, launders a real failure into a pass"; retries are counted and surfaced (§11.4.248). Honest boundary §11.4.6 — guarantees the tested bytes are the shipped bytes; does NOT prove them correct (§11.4.108/§11.4.40/§11.4.185), that the deploy reached the intended target (§11.4.200), or that injected config is right (§11.4.254). Classification: universal (§11.4.17); environment ladder, digest scheme and config-injection mechanism are consumer DATA per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4/.1/.6/.10/.35/.108/.111/.151/.200/.235(B)/.246 (its DEPLOY-side complement: §11.4.246 governs how the artifact is BUILT, §11.4.264 that it is not built AGAIN)/.248/.252/.265/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-264-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.264) + recommended gates CM-BUILD-ONCE-PROMOTE-DIGEST + CM-DEPLOY-CONFIG-INJECTED-NOT-BAKED + paired §1.1 mutations (add a second per-environment build, or swap a promoted digest for a mutable tag, or bake an environment endpoint into the build → the gates FAIL; golden-FALSE per §11.4.201(1): one build + N digest-referencing promotions MUST NOT fire either); gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.264. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --rebuild-per-environment, --promote-by-tag, --bake-env-config-at-build, --rebuild-until-green, --skip-digest-pin, --deterministic-build-means-rebuild-is-safe flag.

§11.4.265 — Progressive delivery: traffic-shifted rollout gated by automated analysis on SLO and business metrics, with automatic abort to stable (research-derived, 2026-08-20). Compact summary: a release reaches users by a bounded-blast-radius strategy — CANARY (gradual weighted traffic shift, smallest blast radius, old+new run concurrently so schema/compat must tolerate it) or BLUE-GREEN (full parallel stand-up, one-shot cutover, rollback window; "at the switch, all users hit the new version simultaneously") — never an ungated cutover; for §11.4.239 critical-invariant surfaces the corpus prefers canary with blue-green's instant-undo posture. (B) THE PROMOTE/ABORT DECISION IS AUTOMATED, NOT A HUMAN WATCHING A DASHBOARD — a controller compares the canary against the stable baseline over successive analysis windows and "auto-promotes … or auto-aborts and rolls back all traffic to stable — no human needed"; a manual approval click is explicitly rejected ("Manual LGTM is a rubber stamp with no receipt; under agents it looks like oversight while providing none"), composing §11.4.240 and §11.4.262. (C) THE LOAD-BEARING CLAUSE — THE ANALYSIS MUST INCLUDE BUSINESS METRICS, NOT ONLY INFRASTRUCTURE METRICS; the corpus names infra-only measurement "the classic mistake": "'HTTP error rate is fine' does not tell you the checkout-completion rate quietly dropped 2% … the failures that matter most are business failures that look technically green. So the analysis must include business KPIs, or it re-creates the 'green pipeline that proves nothing' at the deploy layer." An infra-only canary is a §11.4 PASS-bluff wearing a rollout's uniform. (D) MULTIPLE CONSECUTIVE ANALYSIS WINDOWS, never a single sample (§11.4.50/§11.4.107). (E) POST-DEPLOY SMOKE IS A FAIL-CLOSED PRECONDITION OF TRAFFIC (§11.4.252/§11.4.254). (F) THE DEPLOY GATE IS INDEPENDENT OF THE MERGE GATE — "'got it merged' is not 'got it shipped.'" (G) THRESHOLDS AND WINDOW COUNTS ARE CONSUMER DATA, NEVER CONSTITUTIONAL LITERALS — the corpus writes its abort criterion with UNFILLED placeholders ("below X% or p99 exceeds Y ms … (thresholds illustrative — set from your SLOs)") and warns "A release gate that quotes a stale requirement is itself a bluff." Honest boundary §11.4.6 — bounds blast radius and automates undo; does NOT prove the release correct, does NOT replace §11.4.185 manual QA, and does NOT apply where no traffic-shiftable surface exists (honest §11.4.3 SKIP-with-reason, never a fabricated canary). The corpus's progressive delivery is exclusively traffic-shifting; it is silent on feature flags / dark launch / kill switches / shadow traffic and none is mandated, and it contains no "a deploy you cannot roll back is not a deploy" claim. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4/.1/.3/.5/.6/.35/.50/.69/.107/.185/.201/.235/.238/.239/.240/.252/.254/.262/.264/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-265-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.265) + recommended gates CM-PROGRESSIVE-DELIVERY-STRATEGY-DECLARED + CM-CANARY-ANALYSIS-INCLUDES-BUSINESS-METRIC + paired §1.1 mutations (strip every business metric leaving infra-only, or replace the automated abort with a manual approval → the gates FAIL; golden-FALSE: a non-routable artifact with an honest SKIP-with-reason MUST NOT fire either); gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.265. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --full-cutover-ok, --infra-metrics-suffice, --manual-approval-is-the-gate, --single-window-analysis, --skip-smoke-before-traffic, --merge-implies-deploy flag.

§11.4.266 — Claim-vs-reality ledger: every ADVERTISED capability is a row, typed from the closed bluff-type vocabulary, and a capability with no passing challenge is a release blocker (research-derived, 2026-08-20). Compact summary: corpus module 35 names this the FIRST and cheapest of its portfolio gates — "A shared claim-vs-reality ledger per repo … every advertised capability is a row with a Bluff type and a severity, and a capability with no passing challenge is a release blocker. This makes stubbed-core, byte-identical-fork, and config-present-but-unwired visible and blocking rather than discovered later." THE DIRECTION OF ENUMERATION IS THE POINT and is what no existing anchor supplies: §11.4.25's coverage ledger, §11.4.52's autonomous-path column and §11.4.169's test-type column are all keyed on FEATURES the project knows it has; a claim-vs-reality ledger is keyed on what the project ADVERTISES (README, docs, API/CLI surface, §11.4.254 capability matrix, §11.4.259 badges), so a claim with no feature row is invisible to a feature-keyed ledger by construction — §11.4.266 adds a keying, not a restatement (§11.4.227). (A) ONE LEDGER PER REPOSITORY, claim-side; the advertised-surface set is consumer DATA (§11.4.35). (B) EACH ROW CARRIES A BLUFF TYPE FROM THE CLOSED SEVEN-MEMBER VOCABULARY — green-but-broken / coverage-theater / rubber-stamp-verified / stubbed-core / doc-vs-code-drift / config-present-but-unwired / byte-identical-fork — plus a severity (the corpus states NO severity scale; consumer DATA). (C) THE TYPE IS A ROUTING KEY TO A COUNTER-GATE — "it turns 'another bug' into 'another instance of type T,' which is what lets you install one gate that kills the whole type" — each type binding to the anchor that already owns its counter (§11.4.108/.262, §1.1/§11.4.224(C), §11.4.146(D3)/.240/.249, §11.4.27/.254, §11.4.106/.186/.227, §11.4.124/.196(F), §11.4.251); §11.4.266 makes the ROUTING mandatory, it does not restate the counters. (D) THE RELEASE-BLOCKING RULE — a row whose challenge is absent, never executed, or executed only against a stale artifact fingerprint blocks exactly as a FAILING challenge does (§11.4.135/§11.4.236 absence-blocks-as-FAIL, applied to the claim side). (E) THE ESCAPE HALF is §11.4.238's, cited not duplicated. PROVENANCE CORRECTION (§11.4.6): secondary project documentation has called this material an "FP-1 … FP-14" catalogue, an "L-1 … L-16" list, and an "F-1 … F-31" bluff taxonomy; measured against the tracked corpus, no F-n, FP-n or L-n identifier exists in any of the nine modules (control-needle proven — the same scan returns the real ATM-277/ATM-343/LVA-098/ITE-6/API-33 ids and 0 for a negative control). The real structures are a 14-row numbered failure-pattern table, a 6-bullet portfolio-gate list, and this 7-member bluff-type vocabulary; those labels MUST NOT be cited as corpus wording. Honest boundary §11.4.6 — proves every ADVERTISED capability has a passing challenge; does NOT prove the challenge strong (§11.4.245/§11.4.107(10)/§1.1), the capability bug-free (§11.4.118), or enumerate capabilities nobody advertised (claim-keyed and feature-keyed ledgers are complements; running one leaves an honestly-stated blind side). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4/.1/.6/.25/.27/.35/.52/.106/.108/.115(F)/.118/.124/.135/.146(D3)/.169/.186/.196(F)/.197/.212/.224/.226/.227/.236/.238/.240/.245/.249/.251/.254/.257/.259/.262/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-266-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.266) + recommended gates CM-CLAIM-REALITY-LEDGER-COMPLETE + CM-UNCHALLENGED-CAPABILITY-BLOCKS-RELEASE + CM-LEDGER-ROW-TYPED-FROM-CLOSED-VOCABULARY + paired §1.1 mutations (advertise a capability with no row, blank a row's challenge, or retype a row to an invented type → the gates FAIL; golden-FALSE: a fully-populated ledger with fresh candidate-fingerprinted PASS verdicts MUST NOT fire any); gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.266. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --ledger-optional, --unchallenged-capability-ok, --untyped-ledger-row, --invent-bluff-type, --feature-ledger-suffices, --advertise-without-row flag.

§11.4.267 — Shared attempt record + converge-on-evidence / escalate-on-stall: a failed approach is never silently retried, and a non-converging loop is itself a signal (research-derived, 2026-08-20). Compact summary: corpus module 34 — "No memory of what was tried → the loop repeats itself. Agents re-attempt discarded approaches. Fix: a shared record of attempts and outcomes (in the task state / PR thread), so a failed approach is not silently retried; and an escalation rule — after N failed iterations, stop and route to a human or a stronger model, rather than burning cycles." The premise that makes it structural: "The agent has no memory of prior attempts. It will re-implement the same wrong fix because it does not remember the last three" — so the record is externalised memory, not a convenience log. WHAT THIS IS NOT (the §11.4.227 boundary, stated first): §11.4.147 governs a CRASHED agent, §11.4.232(C) a HUNG long-op, §11.4.21 the TERMINAL exhaustion audit — none governs a LIVE, HEALTHY, PROGRESSING loop re-walking a dead end its predecessor already proved dead; §11.4.267 closes exactly that gap. (A) A SHARED, DURABLE ATTEMPT RECORD across every actor that may take the next attempt (successor agent, §11.4.147 respawn, §11.4.176 other track); the §11.4.93 DB and §11.4.116 event stream are the substrates and MUST be reused, not duplicated. (B) CONSULTED BEFORE ACTING — the record is READ before the next attempt; a recorded-failed approach is not silently retried, and a re-attempt is permitted only on a recorded material difference (changed precondition, corrected §11.4.199 sequence, new §11.4.115(F) RED); a written-but-never-read record is the §11.4.196(F) configured-≠-in-use failure applied to agent memory. (C) CONVERGE ON EVIDENCE — "Each loop must monotonically reduce uncertainty", on a SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH ("the verifier's build exit 1 beats the producer's fluent paragraph every time") with CONCRETE FAILURE FEEDBACK ("feed the exact captured error (argv, exit code, failing assertion, the offending line) back as the next input"). (D) STALL IS A DETECTED SIGNAL WITH A BOUNDED RETRY AND AN ESCALATION PATH — two named signatures ("the same criterion red after N tries", "oscillating diffs"), the stall itself tracked and trended, and the forbidden shape explicit: "an unbounded retry loop with no ground-truth feedback and no escalation — that is how agents thrash expensively while appearing busy." Escalation composes §11.4.231 (re-tier), §11.4.101, §11.4.66/§11.4.21. (E) N IS CONSUMER DATA, NEVER A CONSTITUTIONAL LITERAL — the corpus leaves N symbolic in every occurrence and states no numeric, wall-clock, token or cost bound; this anchor mandates that a bound EXISTS and is DECLARED, never a value. (F) THE LOOP'S DONE-CONDITION — not satisfied while an attempt is in-flight with no outcome, and every escalation reaches a terminal disposition (§11.4.87/.94/.97/.126/.147/.197). Honest boundary §11.4.6 — prevents the RECORDED dead end from being re-walked; does NOT guarantee convergence (an escalation is an honest outcome), prove any attempt competent, or substitute for §11.4.102. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.6/.20/.21/.35/.66/.70/.87/.93/.94/.97/.101/.102/.115(F)/.116/.126/.146/.147/.176/.187/.196(F)/.197/.199/.231/.232(C)/.240/.249/.262/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-267-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.267) + recommended gates CM-ATTEMPT-RECORD-SHARED-AND-CONSULTED + CM-STALL-BOUNDED-AND-ESCALATED + paired §1.1 mutations (dispatch blind without reading the record, remove the retry bound, or record an escalation with no terminal disposition → the gates FAIL; golden-FALSE: a loop converging on attempt 2 with both attempts recorded MUST NOT fire either); gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.267. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --no-attempt-record, --record-without-reading, --unbounded-retry-loop, --retry-same-failed-approach, --stall-without-escalation, --invent-N-and-cite-corpus flag.

EXTENSIONS landed 2026-08-20 (existing anchors strengthened — full text in Constitution.md; research-derived from the tracked AI-curriculum corpus docs/research/ai_curriculum_2026_08/source_corpus/, modules 27/30/31/32/33/34/35). Five anchors gain clauses rather than five new numbers being minted (§11.4.227 — a rule already covered is EXTENDED, never restated as a fresh anchor). §11.4.233(G) — dependency-pointer preflight fails closed on THREE classes, not one: clause (A)'s Plane-R catalogue named only drifted submodule pointers; the catalogue is extended with ABSENT/UNINITIALISED (declared but not checked out — an empty directory read as a satisfied dependency) and UNFETCHABLE (the recorded pointer resolves on NO configured remote — the not our ref condition, invisible in the checkout that created it and failing only on a fresh clone). Corpus module 35 row 5: "The fix is real and merged in a submodule, but the top-level image ships the old pointer, so the bug returns from a clean checkout"; its prescription is a HARD gate — "A hard preflight gate that fails the build … 'the fix is in the submodule but the image shipped old code' must be impossible, not caught after" — with four mechanical steps: "resolves every submodule pointer, verifies it matches the intended ref, fails closed on a missing checkout, and — for the truly load-bearing components — asserts a runtime signature on the flashed/deployed artifact." General principle: "fail closed when an input to your correctness is unverifiable … a missing thing silently read as a present, correct thing." Gate CM-DEPENDENCY-POINTER-PREFLIGHT-FAIL-CLOSED. §11.4.242 — delta debugging: bisection is not only over commits. §11.4.242's own honest boundary limits it to "the INTRODUCING COMMIT of a MONOTONE regression", leaving defects with no known-good commit and defects whose discriminating variable is not a commit. Corpus module 33 supplies the generalisation and its containment direction — git bisect is delta debugging over commits, not the reverse — with the enumerated axes "Bisect a config … Bisect the data … Bisect the change set … Bisect dependencies" (plus feature flags; STATE is NOT an enumerated axis and is not claimed as one). A non-monotone or non-commit-discriminated failure is narrowed to a MINIMAL failing case which becomes the §11.4.115 RED test's input, under a "stable, fast, unambiguous test predicate" run repeatedly where the failure is noisy (§11.4.50/§11.4.248). Gate CM-DELTA-DEBUG-MINIMAL-CASE. §11.4.260(B)(2) — observability: the mechanism, not only the property. The clause NAMED observability; three mechanical requirements were missing. Corpus module 33: the three complementary signals — structured logs ("queryable key–value events (JSON), not prose strings"), metrics, traces — plus crash/ANR analytics; a correlation id threading all of them that MUST survive async boundaries ("I make sure the correlation ID survives the queue … or I lose the thread exactly where the hard bugs live"), pivoting metric → trace → log under "narrow before you theorize"; and crash-free users/sessions as a release gate — "the honest health signal I gate releases on … I treat a new signature after a deploy as a release-blocker even at low volume" (binding §11.4.152 and §11.4.265). Thresholds are consumer DATA — the corpus states none and cautions that crash-free thresholds "change over time and by version". CITATION DISCIPLINE (§11.4.6): the corpus does NOT use "observability triad" or "three pillars", and contains NO claim of the form "a system you cannot observe in production cannot be verified in production" — what it licenses is narrower: "Observability turns 'sometimes' into a query … the system must be built to explain itself." Gates CM-CORRELATION-ID-THREADED-ACROSS-BOUNDARIES + CM-CRASH-FREE-RELEASE-GATE. §11.4.169 — suite shape: the shape is a consequence, and the generating rule is push-tests-down. §11.4.169 freezes WHICH KINDS of test must exist but not WHERE a behaviour is tested. Corpus module 30 REFUSES to prescribe a shape ("the shape is a consequence, not a goal"; the trophy-is-always-correct answer is explicitly marked WRONG; no numeric ratios are stated) and instead mandates the generating rule: "push each test as far down as it will still catch the bug; if a high-level test fails with no lower-level test failing, add the lower-level one and consider deleting the redundant high-level one." Two named anti-patterns bound it — the ice-cream cone ("slow, flaky, still misses unit edges") at the top and mock-heavy unit theater ("it never touched the system it claims to test", "can hit 100% coverage … and still catch nothing") at the bottom, the latter already forbidden by §11.4.27(A) and §11.4.224(C). Gate CM-SUITE-SHAPE-DECLARED-AND-PUSHED-DOWN. §11.4.148 — slicing: an item too big to test is an item whose "done" cannot be proven. §11.4.148 governs an item's integrity once it exists and §11.4.239(B) its acceptance criteria; neither governs SIZE AND SHAPE, the upstream cause of the reopen they then have to catch. Corpus module 27: "INVEST (Bill Wake) … Independent, Negotiable, Valuable, Estimable, Small, Testable" — and, stated by the corpus itself, only TWO letters are defined: "the two load-bearing letters are Small and Testable, and they are linked: an item too big to test end-to-end is an item whose 'done' you cannot prove, so it closes on partial evidence and reopens on the untested part." Vertical slices only — the horizontal slice "is never independently valuable or testable"; three amigos and specification by example pin the criteria before code; and under agents "a big fuzzy item is one an agent will report 'done' on with nothing to check it against" (§11.4.240/§11.4.262 entering at the item-definition layer). CITATION DISCIPLINE (§11.4.6): the corpus names "SMART Tasks" once, in a source title only, never expands or mandates it — no SMART obligation may be grounded on it; and "small" is testability-in-isolation, NOT a point/hour/line count (the corpus states no size metric). Gate CM-ITEM-SLICED-SMALL-AND-TESTABLE. All five gates + their paired §1.1 mutations = gate-code, a separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6/§11.4.227). The literals 11.4.233 / 11.4.242 / 11.4.260 / 11.4.169 / 11.4.148 are unchanged — their propagation gates stay GREEN.

§11.4.268 — Tamper-evident evidence chain + periodic anchor record: deletion, reordering, and tail-truncation of an accepted evidence record are DETECTED, not merely content-mutation (spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26)

Compact summary: every accepted evidence record (the §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdict store, the §11.4.116 event stream, or an equivalent evidence-bearing ledger a consuming project maintains) MUST be tamper-evident against THREE distinct attacks, not only the content-mutation attack §11.4.115(F)/§11.4.226 already cover: (1) deleting an entry, (2) reordering two entries, and (3) truncating the tail — and the mechanism that detects each MUST be named, because a forward-hash-chain alone detects (1) and (2) only when the chain is left internally inconsistent, and does NOT detect an attacker who deletes an entry and then RECOMPUTES the chain forward from that point (spec.md:132, FR-029's own measured boundary: "an actor who deletes an entry and then recomputes the whole chain is NOT detected by the chain alone (0.43 s for 100k entries); that case is detected only against an anchor") — closing that gap requires a SEPARATE periodic anchor record the producer can append to but never rewrite (spec.md:137–139, FR-037–039). The mandate (ALL hold): (A) EVERY STATE-CHANGE ENTRY WITHIN THE DECLARED COVERED-CALL SET, NO CITED-READ VOLUME CREEP. Scope (§11.4.35): this clause binds the enforcement system's own tool-call ledger under §002-anti-slop-enforcement (spec.md:129, FR-026 is written against THAT ledger, not every write/exec/deploy on the host) — the consuming project declares its own concrete COVERED-CALL SET (which state-changing operations this evidence chain tracks) as DATA per §11.4.35; §11.4.268 does NOT itself mandate a fleet-wide "every write/exec/deploy anywhere produces a chain entry" obligation, only that within the declared set, coverage is complete and cited-vs-uncited is honoured. A state-changing call WITHIN the declared covered-call set (write, exec, deploy — spec.md:129, FR-026) MUST produce a matching chain entry naming it. An UNCITED read of the store — a read whose result backs no claim — MUST NOT be required to produce an entry (spec.md:130, FR-027: "this guards against both volume creep and a gate that refuses correct behaviour"); a read result CITED in support of a claim MUST have a chain entry (spec.md:131, FR-028). The distinction is CITED-vs-UNCITED, never present-vs-absent-in-storage. (B) CHAIN VERIFICATION DETECTS DELETION + REORDER WITHOUT RECONSTRUCTION. Deleting one entry, or reordering two entries, WITHOUT reconstructing the chain that follows, MUST be reported by the integrity check (spec.md:132, FR-029) — mutation-detection of a single entry's content alone does NOT satisfy this; the check walks the FULL forward chain, recomputing every link, not spot-checking. This clause's own honest limit is stated in (C): a reconstructed (recomputed) chain defeats (B) alone. (C) THE ANCHOR IS THE ONLY MECHANISM THAT CATCHES A RECOMPUTED-FORWARD DELETION OR A TAIL TRUNCATION. A periodic ANCHOR entry — chain head digest AND entry count, recorded to a location the producer can APPEND to but NOT REWRITE (spec.md:137, FR-037: "the count is what makes wholesale store deletion a detected absence rather than silence") — MUST be written on a declared interval. The anchor's STRENGTH is recorded honestly as mechanism (cryptographically or append-only enforced — a git commit history, a write-once object store, or the consuming project's declared equivalent per §11.4.35) or policy (procedurally enforced only); mechanism MUST NOT be claimed without evidence that rewriting is mechanically prevented, and absent that evidence the recorded strength is policy (spec.md:138, FR-038). Verification against the anchor detects a tail truncation that chain-alone verification PASSES cleanly on (spec.md:139, FR-039: "the two results together are the evidence that the anchor, not the chain, carries this property") — so "the chain verified clean" is NEVER sufficient on its own; a verdict of tamper-free MUST name BOTH the chain-walk result AND the anchor cross-check result. (D) VERIFICATION THAT CANNOT COMPLETE REFUSES — IT NEVER REPORTS THE CHAIN INTACT. A chain truncated mid-verification, or an anchor location unreachable at verification time, yields REFUSAL (an UNVERIFIED status, per §11.4.201's conservative-safe-default-on-an-unresolvable-signal), never a "clean pass by default" (spec.md:133, FR-030). "Could not verify" and "verified clean" are never the same reported state. Honest boundary (§11.4.6). §11.4.268 detects deletion, reordering, and tail-truncation of ALREADY-RECORDED entries. It does NOT prove the first entry ever written was itself truthful — the entry's CONTENT is only as trustworthy as the harness that wrote it (§11.4.115(F) machine-written, §11.4.240 producer≠verifier for who may write it), and it does NOT make the underlying evidence CORRECT, only tamper-EVIDENT. Where a project already operates a content-addressed, atomically-committed store for a related purpose (the §11.4.207 continuum resume engine), §11.4.268 REUSES that mechanism's chaining primitive rather than inventing a second one — a consuming project MUST NOT stand up a parallel, divergent chain implementation when one already satisfies this anchor's properties (§11.4.227 extend-don't-duplicate applied to mechanism reuse, not only to rule text). Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — a platform-neutral tamper-evidence discipline reusable by any project maintaining an evidence/verdict ledger; the consuming project supplies its concrete chain implementation, anchor location, anchor-interval, AND covered-call set (clause A — which state-changing operations this project's evidence chain tracks) as DATA per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.115(F) (the verdict entries THIS anchor chains) / §11.4.116 (the event stream this anchor may chain) / §11.4.201 (verification-cannot-complete → refuse, the conservative-safe default) / §11.4.205 (durable/atomic/ append-only construction discipline) / §11.4.207 (reuse its content-addressed store where one already exists) / §11.4.226 (evidence-class-at-closure — a chain entry's own class must match the defect layer it backs) / §11.4.262 (machine-created evidence at every gate — the chain entries this anchor makes tamper-evident ARE the machine-created evidence artefacts §11.4.262 requires; §11.4.268 is the integrity property that keeps those artefacts trustworthy AFTER the moment of PASS, not merely present at it) / §9.2 (a genuine tamper finding is itself a destructive-op-class event requiring the §9.2 backup-and-investigate protocol) / §1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-268-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.268 present as a block-start, exactly-once per governance file, lockstep content-hash equality across the mirror set per §11.4.227(B)) + recommended mechanism gates CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-DELETE-REORDER-DETECTED (chain-walk verification detects a deleted or reordered entry left internally inconsistent) + CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-ANCHOR-CATCHES-RECOMPUTE-AND-TRUNCATION (an anchor record exists on the declared interval, carries head-digest + entry-count + an honest mechanism/ policy strength, and anchor-cross-check verification detects a recomputed-forward deletion and a tail truncation that chain-alone verification misses) + CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-INCOMPLETE-VERIFICATION-REFUSES (a chain or anchor that cannot be reached/completed at verification time yields REFUSAL, never a default clean pass) + paired §1.1 mutations (delete a middle entry and recompute the chain forward from that point using only internal chain data → chain-alone verification MUST still PASS [proving the gap the anchor exists to close] while anchor cross-check MUST FAIL; truncate the tail and verify chain-alone → MUST PASS while anchor cross-check MUST FAIL; make the anchor location unreachable → verification MUST report UNVERIFIED, never PASS; golden-FALSE per §11.4.201(1): an untampered chain with a fresh, reachable, correctly-computed anchor MUST NOT fire any of the three gates). Gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped (§11.4.6 / §11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.268 (pending landing). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context.
